These principles promote **respect, honesty, and responsibility**, ensuring that technology connects people meaningfully rather than divides or harms them.\n\nOn platforms like [Discord](https://discord.com/), where thousands of communities gather daily to share ideas, play games, or collaborate, these values are especially important. Every message, reaction, and interaction contributes to the tone and culture of a server — whether it’s a small friend group or a large public community.\n\n**Key Principles of Digital Morality on Discord**\n\n- **Respect for Privacy:**\nNever share another user’s personal details, screenshots, or direct messages without consent. Protect your own privacy too — review your account settings and stay cautious with what you post or share.\n\n- **Avoidance of Harm:**\nBe mindful of how your words and actions affect others. Avoid toxic behavior, harassment, or spreading negativity. Discord’s tools — such as moderation bots, slowmode, and report systems — exist to keep spaces safe.\n\n- **Intellectual Property Rights:**\nGive credit where it's due. Whether sharing artwork, resources, or music, always acknowledge the original creator. Avoid reposting or distributing copyrighted materials without permission. If you do not have explicit permission, always link back to the original source so that proper credit is maintained, the creator retains control over their work, and viewers can engage with the content through the creator's own platform. [Courts](https://www.dmlp.org/legal-guide/linking-copyrighted-materials) generally agree that linking to another website does not infringe the copyrights of that site. This means linking is always the safest and most respectful option when you cannot obtain direct permission.\n\n- **Honesty and Transparency:**\nBe genuine in your communication. Avoid impersonating others, spreading false information, or manipulating messages for attention. Trust in Discord communities grows from clarity and truthfulness.\n\n- **Respect for Server Rules and Resources:**\nEach Discord server has its own guidelines. Read them, follow them, and respect moderators who enforce them. Misusing bots, spamming, or exploiting loopholes undermines the harmony of a community.\n\n- **Responsible Sharing:**\nThink before you post. Screenshots, images, or jokes can travel fast — and once shared, they can’t easily be taken back.
